HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000007/B001","root_000291/B006","root_000357/B002","root_001422/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0287846dfc587f4f432a","text":"5. **The family/body cord is externalized as a punitive lineage line.** Branch evidence: `ح ب ل` B006, pregnancy extending in the womb; `ر ح م` B003, womb; `ح م ل` B002, inward bearing; `ء ب و` B001, fatherhood/nurture; `م س د` B001, twisted rope. Structural cue: the packet moves from mercy/womb and fatherhood to wife/carrying and then a cord at the neck. Assigned role: the rope becomes a displaced kinship/gestation cord. Abductive move: I supply the anatomical-social mapping: a family line that should carry mercy/life is twisted into a visible bond of liability. Reading change: the final ayah can be read as an anti-lineage emblem, where relation itself has become the cord of punishment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000284/B001","root_000291/B008","root_001422/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0df6f9a7c4888994e61e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:5:2\",\"root_000284\",[\"B001\"]],[\"111:5:3\",\"root_000291\",[\"B008\"]],[\"111:5:5\",\"root_001422\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Boyun ve süs çağrışımları, takı yerini kaba ve bükülmüş bir ipin aldığı bir tersine dönüş kurar; toplumsal gösteriş kısıtlamaya çevrilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000284/B001","root_000291/B001","root_001422/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_13cd3e91193e6a6628ed","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:5:2\",\"root_000284\",[\"B001\"]],[\"111:5:3\",\"root_000291\",[\"B001\"]],[\"111:5:5\",\"root_001422\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Boyundaki bükülmüş ip, önceki taşıma sahnesini tamamlayan bir koşum ve yöneltme düzeneğine dönüşür; etkin taşıyıcı artık bağlanan ve götürülen bedendir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000284/B001","root_000291/B002","root_001110/B002","root_001422/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_15d55783f1983226d697","text":"2. **Covenant and protection are inverted into constriction.** Branch evidence: `ح ب ل` B002, covenant/protection/connection; `ج ي د` B001, neck; `م س د` B001, firm twisting; earlier `ر ح م` B002, kinship bond; `غ ن ي` B002, availing/protecting. Structural cue: the packet begins with mercy/kinship and passes through failed wealth-protection before ending with a bond around the throat. Assigned role: the rope supplies the same semantic form as connection, but its local placement makes connection punitive. Abductive move: I infer that bonds can reverse valence when the relation they preserve is destructive. Reading change: the final rope reads as failed covenant or corrupted kinship made bodily tight.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3135bb4757c9b33e3683","text":"No later ayah exists in the clipped focus window, so no later-ayah surprise emerged. Retrospective rereading only sharpened how this final cord gathers earlier hands, earning, fire, and carrying into the endpoint of binding.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000291/B005","root_000880/B005","root_001296/B003","root_001693/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3fb14d1734e566a00a53","text":"4. **The rope is a snare that catches the trapper.** Branch evidence: `ح ب ل` B005, snare that catches and entangles; `ص ل ي` B005, snares and traps; earlier `ي د ي` B001, hand caught in a snare; `س م و` B006, going out to hunt; `ك س ب` B003, predatory/hunting creatures called earners. Structural cue: hunting/trapping branches appear across roots in the packet, while the final image fixes the rope on the woman herself. Assigned role: the rope shifts from tool to consequence. Abductive move: I infer a reversal in which the one participating in capture/entanglement is herself caught. Reading change: the final line becomes a trap-reversal scene, not only a static collar.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4e1145e4fd9842d09081","text":"5. **The rope condenses prolonged labor into consequence.** **Lexical evidence:** `مَسَد`, root `م س د`, branch `B003`, denotes sustained exertion through night travel; `حَطَب`, root `ح ط ب`, branch `B002`, supplies the night wood-gatherer; and `حَمَّالَة`, root `ح م ل`, branch `B007`, supplies burdened exertion. The direct material sense remains `م س د` branch `B001`, firm twisting. **Structure:** three consecutive final roots move from repeated carrying, to cord, to twist, while the night/exertion branches supply duration. **Role and abduction:** sustained labor and twisting are supplied; I infer that repeated acts are like strands accumulated into the final rope. **Reading change:** `مِن مَسَد` gives the restraint a temporal as well as material composition: it is spun out of an ongoing practice.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4e51ef3662faed40c4db","text":"1. **A concrete restraint made by skilled twisting.** **Lexical evidence:** `جِيد` has root `ج ي د`, branch `B001` (the neck, especially its front); `حَبْل` has root `ح ب ل`, branch `B001` (an extended cord used for binding or leading); and `مَسَد` has root `م س د`, branch `B001` (fiber, hair, or hide firmly twisted into rope). **Structure:** `فِي` fixes the object at a body location and `مِن` specifies its material, moving from anatomy to instrument to construction. **Role and abduction:** the packet supplies all three physical components; I infer that a firmly twisted leading cord removes the mobility and agency previously expressed by carrying. **Reading change:** the closing image is mechanically precise: the active carrier is now the body bound and led by her own kind of equipment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000284/B001","root_000291/B008","root_001379/B008","root_001422/B002","root_001422/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5388418c3b46dfc6241c","text":"3. **The neck ornament is converted into coarse rope.** Branch evidence: `ج ي د` B001 includes fine/long neck expressions; `ح ب ل` B008, necklace ornament; `م س د` B002, compactly formed body, especially woman/girl; `م س د` B007, arrangement of hair; earlier `ل ه ب` B008, striking beauty or abundant hair. Structural cue: the surface places `حَبْل` on `جِيد`, the position of adornment, but specifies `مَسَد` rather than precious material. Assigned role: beauty/adornment branches provide the expected social display that is downgraded into twisted fiber. Abductive move: I infer a reversal from ornament to humiliation because the local material is coarse rope and the context is firewood-bearing. Reading change: the ayah reads not simply as bondage but as anti-adornment: status display becomes punitive equipment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000291/B006","root_001422/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7148a5d2ef7a0f1e96e8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:5:3\",\"root_000291\",[\"B006\"]],[\"111:5:5\",\"root_001422\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Gebelik bağı çağrışımı, yaşam ve merhamet taşıması gereken hane ilişkisini boyunda görünen cezalandırıcı bir soy bağına tersine çevirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8d978bb040adf1eaa2f9","text":"3. **The final cord closes a hunter-to-captive reversal.** **Lexical evidence:** `حَبْل`, root `ح ب ل`, branch `B005`, is a snare that catches and can figuratively denote causes of death; branch `B011` adds a calamity or cunning that ensnares its owner. Earlier `كَسَبَ`, root `ك س ب`, branch `B003`, evokes hunting creatures called earners, `يَدَا`, root `ي د ي`, branch `B001`, includes a hand caught in a snare, and `ص ل ي` branch `B005` names traps. **Structure:** hands act, acquisitions accumulate, fuel is gathered, and only at the end does the neck reveal who has been caught. **Role and abduction:** the packet supplies active hunting and passive capture images; I infer that the acquisition-and-gathering system has reversed direction onto its operators. **Reading change:** the rope is the closure of an entrapment plot, not an arbitrary accessory to punishment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9aa6b838ace140532841","text":"2. **The necklace position is converted from adornment to constraint.** **Lexical evidence:** root `ج ي د`, branch `B001`, includes the long or fine neck; root `ح ب ل`, branch `B008`, names a necklace ornament called *habalah*; and root `م س د`, branch `B007`, concerns the fine arrangement or bearing of hair. The woman-image also activates `غ ن ي` branch `B005` from `111:2`, a woman whose husband or beauty makes adornment unnecessary. **Structure:** the cord occupies the conventional neck/ornament zone but is specified as twisted rope. **Role and abduction:** neck beauty, ornament, and grooming are supplied branch images; I infer a positional substitution in which restraint takes the place of display. **Reading change:** `فِي جِيدِهَا` carries an adornment-reversal: the site of social presentation becomes the site where consequence is publicly worn.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000291/B002","root_001422/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a43bada40112e11fff33","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:5:3\",\"root_000291\",[\"B002\"]],[\"111:5:5\",\"root_001422\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Koruma ve bağ kurma işlevi taşıyan ip, başarısız hane ve akrabalık bağlarını bedende daraltıcı bir yükümlülüğe çevirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000007/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b54e6df796326515bc6e","text":"- As the final ayah, no later ayah in the packet newly activates it. The retrospective pass instead clarified how earlier ayat converge here: fatherhood (`ء ب و` B001), wife/person (`م ر ء` B001), carrying (`ح م ل` B001/B002/B003), and firewood (`ح ط ب` B001/B003) all terminate in a visible cord. - The most surprising backward relation is with the basmalah. `ر ح م` B002/B003 and `ح ب ل` B002/B006 show that relation, womb, covenant, and cord are not decorative side branches; they form a failed-bond network that the final rope makes bodily. - The hunting/trap reading remained exploratory but became more coherent after rereading the whole packet: `س م و` B006, `ك س ب` B003, `ص ل ي` B005, and `ح ب ل` B005 form a distributed capture vocabulary. The weak link is that some hunting branches are remote from the surface forms, but the final rope supplies a visible mechanism for entanglement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000284/B001","root_000291/B001","root_000335/B001","root_000357/B006","root_001422/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d7aa629ae63f9ce7379e","text":"1. **The final object closes the fuel-carrying mechanism as binding gear.** Branch evidence: `ج ي د` B001, neck/front of neck; `ح ب ل` B001, extended cord for binding or leading; `م س د` B001, twisted rope; earlier `ح م ل` B006, carrying gear/strap; `ح ط ب` B001, gathered fuel. Structural cue: after she is named a carrier, the next ayah locates a rope at the neck. Assigned role: rope becomes the harness or binding apparatus of the carrying scene. Abductive move: I infer that the neck-rope is the visible equipment that attaches the carrier to the carried burden and its consequence. Reading change: the ayah is not a separate punishment detail; it completes the material logistics of carrying fuel.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ebe1342ba8272886ed46","text":"4. **A bond of protection persists only as a binding liability.** **Lexical evidence:** root `ح ب ل`, branch `B002`, is covenant, protection, connection, or a means of access. It is activated by the packet's relational terms: opening `ر ح م` branch `B002` (kinship), `أَبِي` from root `ء ب و` branch `B001` (fatherhood), and `ٱمْرَأَتُهُ` from root `م ر ء` branch `B001` (wife/woman). **Structure:** father-name and spouse connect a household whose wealth fails to avail; the only explicit connection left at the end is a cord at the neck. **Role and abduction:** familial bonds and protective-covenant scope are supplied; I infer that failed protection does not erase connection but converts it into shared exposure and restraint. **Reading change:** `حَبْل` can image the household bond turned inside out: attachment remains, but no longer as security.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001422/B001","root_001422/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_eee3982dd1c73132c769","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:5:5\",\"root_001422\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İpin uzun uğraş çağrışımı, süregelen taşıma eylemlerini tek tek lifler gibi biriktirip son kısıtlamayı zaman içinde örer.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000291/B005","root_000291/B011"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f0965422306a701c9648","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"111:5:3\",\"root_000291\",[\"B005\",\"B011\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Tuzak işlevi öne çıkarıldığında, başkalarını yakalayan edinme ve toplama düzeninin sonunda kendi taşıyıcısını yakaladığı düşünülebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
